Error Code Search
Nintendo Switch

ACCOUNT [2124-xxxx]

For application developers: Please refer to ACCOUNT library documentation from NintendoSDK NX Add-On for details. (Features > System > Account Library > Error Codes)

https://developer.nintendo.com/html/online-docs/nx-en/g1kr9vj6-en/Packages/SDK/NintendoSDK/Documents/Package/contents/Pages/Page_187703099.html
